第二评判人甄别页：main_v1（106 组）

请按论文 VI-A 的书面标准，独立地给每一组选一个判定：bug、legitimate 或 tool-artifact。选了 legitimate 或 tool-artifact 时，可以再选一个例外类别；备注框写一句理由，拿不准就写“不确定”。本页不显示任何别人的判定。操作说明见随页发来的 rater2_manual_zh.html。

进度自动存在这个浏览器里（键名 tse2-rater2:triage:main_v1）；换浏览器、换电脑或清除浏览器数据会丢，每次做完请点「下载结果文件」（rater2_judgements.json）留底，换电脑时用「从结果文件恢复」接着做。

组的顺序按固定种子 20261117 随机排列（Python random.Random(20261117).shuffle，先按组名排序再打乱），每次生成都一样。

判定标准原文（论文 VI-A）
A bug when the tested answer is wrong for the language or the protocol and no documented behavior explains it, legitimate when both answers are defensible because the protocol leaves the behavior to the implementation or the difference is documented, and an artifact when the difference comes from the harness's inputs or rules. For R1, which compares a session with a fresh session on identical content, any difference in an answer about project files counts as a bug, whichever session is wrong, unless the harness caused it or the answers differ only in order or printing, only in locations outside the project, or in a result the server documents as best effort under a time budget.
例外类别一览
类别含义用于
L1同样的内容，只是顺序或打印方式不同legitimate
L2差别只在项目以外的位置（已安装的库、标准库的存根文件等）legitimate
L3服务器文档写明在时间预算或后台分析下尽力而为的结果legitimate
L4协议不要求两条路径一致，差别来自看得见的设计legitimate
L5答案按设计取决于行的排布legitimate
L6TypeScript 6 与 TypeScript 7 之间的差别legitimate
L7其他legitimate
A1工具故意埋入的错误（种子变异）带来的副作用tool-artifact
A2工具给的输入让这个比较不成立tool-artifact
A3时机（服务器还没准备好之类）tool-artifact
A4其他tool-artifact
各规则的组数
规则组数
R0 崩溃2
R2 句柄有效2
R4 编辑有效27
R3 路径等价24
R6 TS6 对 TS713
R5 语义不变变换9
R1 历史无关29

Project file `src/utils/requirement-diff.ts`:

```
   1 | import { formatPatch, OMIT_HEADERS, structuredPatch } from 'diff';
   2 | import {
   3 |   extractRequirementsSection,
   4 |   foldRequirementName,
   5 |   normalizeRequirementName,
   6 | } from '../core/parsers/requirement-blocks.js';
     ...
   8 | /** A main-spec requirement block, and how its header matched the delta's. */
   9 | export interface MatchedRequirementBlock {
  10 |   raw: string;
  11 |   /** The header name as the main spec spells it. */
  12 |   name: string;
  13 |   /**
  14 |    * False when the two headers differ only in case or interior spacing. Archive
  15 |    * matches names exactly, so an inexact match is a real problem to report even
  16 |    * though the diff below it is still the one the author meant.
  17 |    */
  18 |   exact: boolean;
  19 | }
     ...
  21 | /**
  22 |  * Find the raw markdown block for a requirement by name in a spec file.
  23 |  *
  24 |  * Delegates to extractRequirementsSection() which already handles code fences,
  25 |  * section boundaries, and requirement header parsing. We just look up by name:
  26 |  * exactly first, then falling back to the shared case/whitespace fold so a
  27 |  * header that differs only in spelling still shows its diff, flagged inexact.
  28 |  *
  29 |  * Returns null if no requirement header matches either way.
  30 |  */
  31 | export function extractRequirementBlock(
  32 |   specContent: string,
  33 |   requirementName: string
  34 | ): MatchedRequirementBlock | null {
  35 |   const parts = extractRequirementsSection(specContent);
  36 |   const targetName = normalizeRequirementName(requirementName);
  37 |   const foldedTarget = foldRequirementName(targetName);
  38 |   let folded: MatchedRequirementBlock | null = null;
     ...
  40 |   for (const block of parts.bodyBlocks) {
  41 |     const name = normalizeRequirementName(block.name);
  42 |     if (name === targetName) {
  43 |       return { raw: block.raw, name, exact: true };
  44 |     }
  45 |     if (!folded && foldRequirementName(name) === foldedTarget) {
  46 |       folded = { raw: block.raw, name, exact: false };
  47 |     }
  48 |   }
     ...
  50 |   return folded;
  51 | }
     ...
  53 | /**
  54 |  * Compute a unified diff between a main-spec requirement block and the delta
  55 |  * block that replaces it. A null main block (new capability) diffs against the
  56 |  * empty string, so every line reads as an addition.
  57 |  *
  58 |  * Uses structuredPatch to retain unified-diff hunk ranges while omitting the
  59 |  * synthetic file headers, since the caller provides its own labeling.
  60 |  */
  61 | export function diffRequirementBlock(baseBlock: string | null, deltaBlock: string, label: string): string {
  62 |   const base = ensureTrailingNewline(baseBlock ?? '');
  63 |   const delta = ensureTrailingNewline(deltaBlock);
  64 |   const patch = structuredPatch(label, label, base, delta);
     ...
  66 |   return formatPatch(patch, OMIT_HEADERS).trimEnd();
  67 | }
     ...
  69 | function ensureTrailingNewline(s: string): string {
  70 |   return s.endsWith('\n') ? s : s + '\n';
  71 | }
     ...
  73 | /**
  74 |  * Build a map from folded RENAMED TO name to the original main-spec name.
  75 |  * Renames apply in source order, so a chain such as A -> B -> C maps C back to
  76 |  * A. That is the block a later MODIFIED C replaces.
  77 |  */
  78 | export function buildRenameMap(renames: Array<{ from: string; to: string }>): Map<string, string> {
  79 |   const map = new Map<string, string>();
  80 |   for (const r of renames) {
  81 |     const from = foldRequirementName(r.from);
  82 |     const original = map.get(from) ?? normalizeRequirementName(r.from);
  83 |     map.delete(from);
  84 |     map.set(foldRequirementName(r.to), original);
  85 |   }
  86 |   return map;
  87 | }
```

# [ts6 6.0.3] hover shows a different text after file rename round trip

Group `ts6-r1-hover-text-h5-b3855d`, signature `ts6|r1_history|hover:text`, case family `H5`.

## Server

- ts6 6.0.3; launch `~/.local/state/fnm_multishells/22713_1790129010272/bin/node temp/lsp_servers/ts6-6.0.3/node_modules/typescript-language-server/lib/cli.mjs --stdio`; entry sha256 146b113929fd41f1; package version 6.0.0; node v24.3.0
- project `ChatGPTNextWeb/NextChat@defdcdb55d85` (corpus, commit defdcdb55d850cd12c4c657eb83729fd66e215c0), language typescript

## Relation violated

History independence (R1): after a sequence of edits that returns the documents to content C, every query must answer as in a fresh session opened on C. Fresh sessions on C agree with each other on this query; the session that went through the history does not.

## Minimal steps (LSP)

1. Start ts6 6.0.3 on the project root; answer workspace/configuration with the settings under Environment.
2. textDocument/didOpen: `open app/locales/index.ts`

4. textDocument/didOpen `app/api/stability.ts`, `app/client/platforms/glm.ts` (the query step opens every target file that is not open), then wait until the server is idle.
5. Query `textDocument/hover` on `app/locales/index.ts` line 56, column 41 (LSP position {line: 55, character: 40}); compare with the same query in a fresh session opened on the final content (expected).

# [ts6 6.0.3] references misses entries after file rename round trip

Group `ts6-r1-references-missing-h5-cc8950`, signature `ts6|r1_history|references:missing`, case family `H5`.

## Server

- ts6 6.0.3; launch `~/.local/state/fnm_multishells/22713_1790129010272/bin/node temp/lsp_servers/ts6-6.0.3/node_modules/typescript-language-server/lib/cli.mjs --stdio`; entry sha256 146b113929fd41f1; package version 6.0.0; node v24.3.0
- project `Fission-AI/OpenSpec@f179ed4e4056` (corpus, commit f179ed4e40567cdb56501ee3f4d09efbcb1557b8), language typescript

## Relation violated

History independence (R1): after a sequence of edits that returns the documents to content C, every query must answer as in a fresh session opened on C. Fresh sessions on C agree with each other on this query; the session that went through the history does not.

## Minimal steps (LSP)

1. Start ts6 6.0.3 on the project root; answer workspace/configuration with the settings under Environment.
2. textDocument/didOpen: `open src/core/shared/skill-generation.ts (then 200 ms)`
3. workspace/willRenameFiles, didClose, rename on disk, workspace/didRenameFiles, didChangeWatchedFiles, didOpen of the new path: `fs_rename src/core/shared/skill-generation.ts -> src/core/shared/skill-generation_rt.ts (then 200 ms)`
4. workspace/willRenameFiles, didClose, rename on disk, workspace/didRenameFiles, didChangeWatchedFiles, didOpen of the new path: `fs_rename src/core/shared/skill-generation_rt.ts -> src/core/shared/skill-generation.ts (then 200 ms)`
5. Timing: send the steps 200 ms apart without waiting, then wait until idle.
6. textDocument/didOpen `src/telemetry/index.ts`, `test/commands/store-git.test.ts` (the query step opens every target file that is not open), then wait until the server is idle.
7. Query `textDocument/references` on `src/core/shared/skill-generation.ts` line 65, column 6 (LSP position {line: 64, character: 5}); compare with the same query in a fresh session opened on the final content (expected).

  10 |   code?: Command;
  11 |   settings?: Command;

  15 |   const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();
     ...
  17 |   useEffect(() => {
  18 |     let shouldUpdate = false;
  19 |     searchParams.forEach((param, name) => {
  20 |       const commandName = name as keyof Commands;
  21 |       if (typeof commands[commandName] === "function") {
  22 |         commands[commandName]!(param);
  23 |         searchParams.delete(name);
  24 |         shouldUpdate = true;
  25 |       }
  26 |     });
     ...
  28 |     if (shouldUpdate) {
  29 |       setSearchParams(searchParams);
  30 |     }
  31 |     // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  32 |   }, [searchParams, commands]);
  33 | }
     ...
  35 | interface ChatCommands {
  36 |   new?: Command;
  37 |   newm?: Command;
  38 |   next?: Command;
  39 |   prev?: Command;
  40 |   clear?: Command;
  41 |   fork?: Command;

  48 | export function useChatCommand(commands: ChatCommands = {}) {
  49 |   function extract(userInput: string) {
  50 |     const match = userInput.match(ChatCommandPrefix);
  51 |     if (match) {
  52 |       return userInput.slice(1) as keyof ChatCommands;
  53 |     }
  54 |     return userInput as keyof ChatCommands;
  55 |   }
     ...

  58 |     const input = extract(userInput);
  59 |     const desc = Locale.Chat.Commands;
  60 |     return Object.keys(commands)
  61 |       .filter((c) => c.startsWith(input))
  62 |       .map((c) => ({
  63 |         title: desc[c as keyof ChatCommands],
  64 |         content: ":" + c,
  65 |       }));
  66 |   }

  69 |     const command = extract(userInput);
  70 |     const matched = typeof commands[command] === "function";
     ...
  72 |     return {
  73 |       matched,
  74 |       invoke: () => matched && commands[command]!(userInput),
  75 |     };
  76 |   }
     ...

```
   1 | """
   2 | API module - exposes the document pipeline over HTTP.
   3 | Thin layer over parser, validator, processor, and storage.
   4 | """
   5 | from parser import batch_parse, parse_file
   6 | from validator import validate_document, ValidationError
   7 | from processor import process_and_save, enrich_document
   8 | from storage import load_record, delete_record, list_records, load_index
     ...
  11 | def handle_upload(paths: list) -> dict:
  12 |     """
  13 |     Accept a list of file paths, run the full pipeline on each,
  14 |     and return a summary of what succeeded and what failed.
  15 |     """
  16 |     results = batch_parse(paths)
  17 |     succeeded = [r for r in results if r["ok"]]
  18 |     failed = [r for r in results if not r["ok"]]
  19 |     return {
  20 |         "uploaded": len(succeeded),
  21 |         "failed": len(failed),
  22 |         "ids": [r["id"] for r in succeeded],
  23 |         "errors": failed,
  24 |     }
     ...
  27 | def handle_get(record_id: str) -> dict:
  28 |     """Fetch a document by ID and return it."""
  29 |     try:
  30 |         return load_record(record_id)
  31 |     except KeyError:
  32 |         return {"error": f"Record {record_id} not found"}
     ...
  35 | def handle_delete(record_id: str) -> dict:
  36 |     """Delete a document by ID."""
  37 |     deleted = delete_record(record_id)
  38 |     if deleted:
  39 |         return {"deleted": record_id}
  40 |     return {"error": f"Record {record_id} not found"}
     ...
  43 | def handle_list() -> dict:
  44 |     """List all document IDs in storage."""
  45 |     return {"records": list_records()}
     ...
  48 | def handle_search(query: str) -> dict:
  49 |     """
  50 |     Simple keyword search over the index.
  51 |     Returns documents whose keyword list overlaps with the query terms.
  52 |     """
  53 |     terms = set(query.lower().split())
  54 |     index = load_index()
  55 |     matches = []
  56 |     for record_id, entry in index.items():
  57 |         keywords = set(entry.get("keywords", []))
  58 |         if terms & keywords:
  59 |             matches.append({
  60 |                 "id": record_id,
  61 |                 "title": entry.get("title", ""),
  62 |                 "matched_keywords": list(terms & keywords),
  63 |             })
  64 |     return {"query": query, "results": matches}
     ...
  67 | def handle_enrich(record_id: str) -> dict:
  68 |     """Re-enrich a document to pick up new cross-references."""
  69 |     try:
  70 |         doc = load_record(record_id)
  71 |     except KeyError:
  72 |         return {"error": f"Record {record_id} not found"}
  73 |     try:
  74 |         validated = validate_document(doc)
  75 |     except ValidationError as e:
  76 |         return {"error": str(e)}
  77 |     enriched_id = process_and_save(validated)
  78 |     return {"enriched": enriched_id}

63. 未选 ts6-r1-typedefinition-lost-h5-712a35 · R1 历史无关 · ts6 · 成员 1 · 步骤 5→3

[ts6 6.0.3] typeDefinition loses its answer after file rename round trip

服务器与项目：

- project `pmndrs/zustand@b57db4f86ef1` (corpus, commit b57db4f86ef179285da216eeb291266da82c361c), language typescript

违反的规则：History independence (R1): after a sequence of edits that returns the documents to content C, every query must answer as in a fresh session opened on C. Fresh sessions on C agree with each other on this query; the session that went through the history does not.

现象：typeDefinition:lost；生成器族/子检查：H5

最少复现步骤

1. Start ts6 6.0.3 on the project root; answer workspace/configuration with the settings under Environment.
2. textDocument/didOpen: `open src/react.ts (then 200 ms)`
3. workspace/willRenameFiles, didClose, rename on disk, workspace/didRenameFiles, didChangeWatchedFiles, didOpen of the new path: `fs_rename src/react.ts -> src/react_rt.ts (then 200 ms)`
4. workspace/willRenameFiles, didClose, rename on disk, workspace/didRenameFiles, didChangeWatchedFiles, didOpen of the new path: `fs_rename src/react_rt.ts -> src/react.ts (then 200 ms)`
5. Timing: send the steps 200 ms apart without waiting, then wait until idle.
6. textDocument/didOpen `src/vanilla/shallow.ts`, `tests/persistSync.test.tsx` (the query step opens every target file that is not open), then wait until the server is idle.
7. Query `textDocument/typeDefinition` on `tests/persistSync.test.tsx` line 566, column 65 (LSP position {line: 565, character: 64}); compare with the same query in a fresh session opened on the final content (expected).

预期与实际的差别（红 = 预期，参照会话的答案；绿 = 实际，被测会话的答案）

   1 | import { formatPatch, OMIT_HEADERS, structuredPatch } from 'diff';
   2 | import {
   3 |   extractRequirementsSection,
   4 |   foldRequirementName,
   5 |   normalizeRequirementName,
   6 | } from '../core/parsers/requirement-blocks.js';
     ...
   8 | /** A main-spec requirement block, and how its header matched the delta's. */
   9 | export interface MatchedRequirementBlock {
  10 |   raw: string;
  11 |   /** The header name as the main spec spells it. */
  12 |   name: string;
  13 |   /**
  14 |    * False when the two headers differ only in case or interior spacing. Archive
  15 |    * matches names exactly, so an inexact match is a real problem to report even
  16 |    * though the diff below it is still the one the author meant.
  17 |    */
  18 |   exact: boolean;
  19 | }
     ...
  21 | /**
  22 |  * Find the raw markdown block for a requirement by name in a spec file.
  23 |  *
  24 |  * Delegates to extractRequirementsSection() which already handles code fences,
  25 |  * section boundaries, and requirement header parsing. We just look up by name:
  26 |  * exactly first, then falling back to the shared case/whitespace fold so a
  27 |  * header that differs only in spelling still shows its diff, flagged inexact.
  28 |  *
  29 |  * Returns null if no requirement header matches either way.
  30 |  */
  31 | export function extractRequirementBlock(
  32 |   specContent: string,
  33 |   requirementName: string
  34 | ): MatchedRequirementBlock | null {
  35 |   const parts = extractRequirementsSection(specContent);
  36 |   const targetName = normalizeRequirementName(requirementName);
  37 |   const foldedTarget = foldRequirementName(targetName);
  38 |   let folded: MatchedRequirementBlock | null = null;
     ...
  40 |   for (const block of parts.bodyBlocks) {
  41 |     const name = normalizeRequirementName(block.name);
  42 |     if (name === targetName) {
  43 |       return { raw: block.raw, name, exact: true };
  44 |     }
  45 |     if (!folded && foldRequirementName(name) === foldedTarget) {
  46 |       folded = { raw: block.raw, name, exact: false };
  47 |     }
  48 |   }
     ...
  50 |   return folded;
  51 | }
     ...
  53 | /**
  54 |  * Compute a unified diff between a main-spec requirement block and the delta
  55 |  * block that replaces it. A null main block (new capability) diffs against the
  56 |  * empty string, so every line reads as an addition.
  57 |  *
  58 |  * Uses structuredPatch to retain unified-diff hunk ranges while omitting the
  59 |  * synthetic file headers, since the caller provides its own labeling.
  60 |  */
  61 | export function diffRequirementBlock(baseBlock: string | null, deltaBlock: string, label: string): string {
  62 |   const base = ensureTrailingNewline(baseBlock ?? '');
  63 |   const delta = ensureTrailingNewline(deltaBlock);
  64 |   const patch = structuredPatch(label, label, base, delta);
     ...
  66 |   return formatPatch(patch, OMIT_HEADERS).trimEnd();
  67 | }
     ...
  69 | function ensureTrailingNewline(s: string): string {
  70 |   return s.endsWith('\n') ? s : s + '\n';
  71 | }
     ...
  73 | /**
  74 |  * Build a map from folded RENAMED TO name to the original main-spec name.
  75 |  * Renames apply in source order, so a chain such as A -> B -> C maps C back to
  76 |  * A. That is the block a later MODIFIED C replaces.
  77 |  */
  78 | export function buildRenameMap(renames: Array<{ from: string; to: string }>): Map<string, string> {
  79 |   const map = new Map<string, string>();
  80 |   for (const r of renames) {
  81 |     const from = foldRequirementName(r.from);
  82 |     const original = map.get(from) ?? normalizeRequirementName(r.from);
  83 |     map.delete(from);
  84 |     map.set(foldRequirementName(r.to), original);
  85 |   }
  86 |   return map;
  87 | }
```

# [gopls v0.23.0] foldingRange changes its answer after transformation T6

Group `gopls-r5-foldingrange-changed-t6-ecacad`, signature `gopls|r5_transforms|foldingRange:changed`, case family `T6`.

## Server

- gopls v0.23.0; launch `temp/lsp_servers/gopls-v0.23.0/gopls serve`; entry sha256 a8b4a156c328a03f; package version -
- project `router-for-me/CLIProxyAPI@673131f57484` (corpus, commit 673131f57484517c3a1eae7e36c4cfa7b9bb4efc), language go

## Relation violated

Semantics-preserving transformation (R5): blank lines, a comment, trailing whitespace, CRLF line ends, the final newline or an inline comment do not change the program, so the answers must agree after mapping positions.

## Minimal steps (LSP)

1. Start gopls v0.23.0 on the project root; answer workspace/configuration with the settings under Environment.
2. Two fresh sessions: one on the original project, one with transformation T6 applied to `sdk/cliproxy/auth/conductor_oauth_alias_suspension_test.go` (1 edit(s), first [73, 35, 73, 35]).
3. Query `textDocument/foldingRange` on `sdk/cliproxy/auth/conductor_oauth_alias_suspension_test.go` for the whole file; compare with the same query on the untransformed project, positions mapped (expected).
